{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01JXxKHQqQq5ytAKDovaAZAt","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_014nBE3zfLtZsjbzTPh6Cv9H","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/text-to-speech-synthesis.md","content":"# Text-to-Speech Synthesis for Dubbing\n\n## Overview\nGenerate natural-sounding speech in target language with precise timing control for video dubbing.\n\n## Libraries & Tools\n- **gTTS (Google Text-to-Speech)**: Simple, free, multilingual TTS\n- **pyttsx3**: Offline, but lower quality\n- **Edge-TTS (Microsoft Edge TTS)**: Free, high-quality, supports rate adjustment\n- **Piper TTS**: Offline, open-source, supports multiple languages\n- **AudioSegment (pydub)**: For audio manipulation and timing\n\n## Installation\n```bash\npip install edge-tts pydub librosa numpy scipy\n```\n\n## Key Concepts for Dubbing\n\n### 1. Duration Matching\nThree strategies to match TTS output to video window:\n- **rate_adjust**: Speed up/slow down TTS audio to fit window duration\n- **pad_silence**: Add silence padding if TTS is shorter\n- **trim**: Cut TTS audio if too long\n\n### 2. Quality Standards\n- Sample rate: 48000 Hz (broadcast standard)\n- Channels: Mono (can mix from stereo)\n- Loudness: ITU-R BS.1770-4 (LUFS measurement)\n- Target LUFS: -23.0 to -19.0 dB\n\n## Code Example: Edge-TTS with Rate Adjustment\n\n```python\nimport asyncio\nimport edge_tts\nfrom pydub import AudioSegment\nimport librosa\nimport soundfile as sf\nimport numpy as np\n\nasync def generate_dubbed_audio(text, target_language, window_duration_sec, output_path):\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate TTS audio and adjust to fit window duration.\n\n    Args:\n        text: Text to synthesize\n        target_language: Language code (e.g., 'ja', 'es', 'fr')\n        window_duration_sec: Duration the audio must fit into\n        output_path: Output WAV file path\n\n    Returns:\n        tuple: (actual_duration_sec, duration_control_method)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Voice selection mapping\n    voices = {\n        'ja': 'ja-JP-NanaNeural',\n        'es': 'es-ES-AlvaroNeural',\n        'fr': 'fr-FR-DeniseNeural',\n        'en': 'en-US-AriaNeural'\n    }\n\n    voice = voices.get(target_language, 'en-US-AriaNeural')\n\n    # Generate TTS with initial rate\n    tts = edge_tts.Communicate(text=text, voice=voice, rate=0)\n    temp_path = '/tmp/tts_temp.mp3'\n\n    await tts.save(temp_path)\n\n    # Load audio\n    audio = AudioSegment.from_mp3(temp_path)\n    duration_sec = len(audio) / 1000.0\n\n    # Determine duration control strategy\n    duration_diff = abs(duration_sec - window_duration_sec)\n    rate_change_percent = ((window_duration_sec - duration_sec) / duration_sec) * 100\n\n    if duration_diff < 0.05:  # Within 50ms tolerance\n        duration_control = \"rate_adjust\"\n        final_duration = duration_sec\n    elif rate_change_percent > 20:  # Need significant speed adjustment\n        # Regenerate with rate adjustment in TTS\n        rate_str = f\"+{int(rate_change_percent)}%\" if rate_change_percent > 0 else f\"{int(rate_change_percent)}%\"\n        tts = edge_tts.Communicate(text=text, voice=voice, rate=rate_change_percent/100)\n        await tts.save(temp_path)\n        audio = AudioSegment.from_mp3(temp_path)\n        duration_control = \"rate_adjust\"\n        final_duration = len(audio) / 1000.0\n    elif duration_sec > window_duration_sec:\n        duration_control = \"trim\"\n        final_duration = window_duration_sec\n    else:\n        duration_control = \"pad_silence\"\n        final_duration = window_duration_sec\n\n    # Export as WAV (will convert to 48kHz mono in audio processing step)\n    audio.export(output_path, format=\"wav\")\n\n    return final_duration, duration_control\n```\n\n## Loudness Measurement\nUse **pyloudnorm** for ITU-R BS.1770-4 compliance:\n\n```python\nimport pyloudnorm as pyln\nimport soundfile as sf\n\ndef measure_loudness(audio_path):\n    \"\"\"Measure loudness in LUFS following ITU-R BS.1770-4.\"\"\"\n    audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n    meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n    loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio)\n    return loudness\n\ndef normalize_loudness(audio_path, target_lufs=-23.0):\n    \"\"\"Normalize audio to target LUFS.\"\"\"\n    audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n    meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n    loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio)\n    loudness_normalized = pyln.normalize.loudness(audio, loudness, target_lufs)\n    return loudness_normalized, sr\n```\n\n## References\n- Edge-TTS: https://github.com/rany2/edge-tts\n- ITU-R BS.1770-4: https://www.itu.int/rec/R-REC-BS.1770-4-201510-I/en\n- Pydub: https://github.com/jiaaro/pydub\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":513,"cache_read_input_tokens":20091,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":513,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"88314caa-04c1-4790-a4d8-058370e9d553","uuid":"21c4c828-55f8-40f7-bff5-f54915e187aa"}

Determine duration control strategy\n    duration_diff = abs(duration_sec - window_duration_sec)\n    rate_change_percent = ((window_duration_sec - duration_sec) / duration_sec) * 100\n\n    if duration_diff < 0.05:  # Within 50ms tolerance\n        duration_control = \"rate_adjust\"\n        final_duration = duration_sec\n    elif rate_change_percent > 20:  # Need significant speed adjustment\n        # Regenerate with rate adjustment in TTS\n        rate_str = f\"+{int(rate_change_percent)}%\" if rate_change_percent > 0 else f\"{int(rate_change_percent)}%\"\n        tts = edge_tts.Communicate(text=text, voice=voice, rate=rate_change_percent/100)\n        await tts.save(temp_path)\n        audio = AudioSegment.from_mp3(temp_path)\n        duration_control = \"rate_adjust\"\n        final_duration = len(audio) / 1000.0\n    elif duration_sec > window_duration_sec:\n        duration_control = \"trim\"\n        final_duration = window_duration_sec\n    else:\n        duration_control = \"pad_silence\"\n        final_duration = window_duration_sec\n\n    # Export as WAV (will convert to 48kHz mono in audio processing step)\n    audio.export(output_path, format=\"wav\")\n\n    return final_duration, duration_control\n```\n\n## Loudness Measurement\nUse **pyloudnorm** for ITU-R BS.1770-4 compliance:\n\n```python\nimport pyloudnorm as pyln\nimport soundfile as sf\n\ndef measure_loudness(audio_path):\n    \"\"\"Measure loudness in LUFS following ITU-R BS.1770-4.\"\"\"\n    audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n    meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n    loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio)\n    return loudness\n\ndef normalize_loudness(audio_path, target_lufs=-23.0):\n    \"\"\"Normalize audio to target LUFS.\"\"\"\n    audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n    meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n    loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio)\n    loudness_normalized = pyln.normalize.loudness(audio, loudness, target_lufs)\n    return loudness_normalized, sr\n```\n\n## References\n- Edge-TTS: https://github.com/rany2/edge-tts\n- ITU-R BS.1770-4: https://www.itu.int/rec/R-REC-BS.1770-4-201510-I/en\n- Pydub: https://github.com/jiaaro/pydub\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

index: segment number\n        - start_sec: start time in seconds (float)\n        - end_sec: end time in seconds (float)\n        - duration_sec: window duration\n        - text: segment text\n    \"\"\"\n    subs = pysrt.load(srt_path)\n    segments = []\n\n    for sub in subs:\n        start_sec = sub.start.total_seconds()\n        end_sec = sub.end.total_seconds()\n\n        segments.append({\n            'index': sub.index,\n            'start_sec': start_sec,\n            'end_sec': end_sec,\n            'duration_sec': end_sec - start_sec,\n            'text': sub.text.strip()\n        })\n\n    return segments\n\ndef time_to_seconds(time_str: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Convert HH:MM:SS,mmm format to seconds.\"\"\"\n    parts = time_str.split(':')\n    hours = int(parts[0])\n    minutes = int(parts[1])\n    seconds_ms = parts[2].split(',')\n    seconds = int(seconds_ms[0])\n    millis = int(seconds_ms[1])\n\n    return hours * 3600 + minutes * 60 + seconds + millis / 1000.0\n\ndef seconds_to_time(seconds: float) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Convert seconds to HH:MM:SS,mmm format.\"\"\"\n    hours = int(seconds // 3600)\n    minutes = int((seconds % 3600) // 60)\n    secs = seconds % 60\n    secs_int = int(secs)\n    millis = int((secs - secs_int) * 1000)\n\n    return f\"{hours:02d}:{minutes:02d}:{secs_int:02d},{millis:03d}\"\n```\n\n### Timing Validation\n```python\ndef validate_timing_placement(window_start_sec: float,\n                              placed_start_sec: float,\n                              tolerance_sec: float = 0.01) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Check if audio placement matches window within tolerance.\n\n    Args:\n        window_start_sec: Original window start time\n        placed_start_sec: Actual placed audio start time\n        tolerance_sec: Maximum allowed deviation (default 10ms)\n\n    Returns:\n        bool: True if within tolerance\n    \"\"\"\n    deviation = abs(window_start_sec - placed_start_sec)\n    return deviation <= tolerance_sec\n\ndef calculate_drift(window_start: float, window_end: float,\n                   placed_start: float, placed_end: float) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate end-time drift in seconds.\n\n    Returns:\n        float: Absolute drift at end position\n    \"\"\"\n    window_duration = window_end - window_start\n    placed_duration = placed_end - placed_start\n    return abs(placed_duration - window_duration)\n```\n\n### SRT Generation\n```python\ndef create_srt_subtitle(index: int, start_sec: float,\n                        end_sec: float, text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Generate single SRT entry.\"\"\"\n    start_time = seconds_to_time(start_sec)\n    end_time = seconds_to_time(end_sec)\n    return f\"{index}\\n{start_time} --> {end_time}\\n{text}\\n\"\n\ndef write_srt_file(segments: List[dict], output_path: str):\n    \"\"\"Write list of segments to SRT file.\"\"\"\n    with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        for seg in segments:\n            entry = create_srt_subtitle(\n                seg['index'],\n                seg['start_sec'],\n                seg['end_sec'],\n                seg['text']\n            )\n            f.write(entry + '\\n')\n```\n\n## References\n- SRT Format: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/SubRip\n- Pysrt: https://github.com/nithinphilips/pysrt\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1605,"cache_read_input_tokens":20604,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1605,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"88314caa-04c1-4790-a4d8-058370e9d553","uuid":"94d45513-b36a-48b0-a684-61683742bdab"}

Load at native sample rate\n    audio, sr = librosa.load(input_path, sr=None, mono=False)\n\n    # Convert to mono if needed\n    if len(audio.shape) > 1:\n        audio = np.mean(audio, axis=0)\n\n    # Resample to 48kHz\n    if sr != 48000:\n        audio = librosa.resample(audio, orig_sr=sr, target_sr=48000)\n\n    # Save\n    sf.write(output_path, audio, 48000, subtype='PCM_16')\n    return audio, 48000\n\ndef time_stretch_audio(audio: np.ndarray, sr: int,\n                       rate: float) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"\n    Time-stretch audio to match duration (changes speed, not pitch).\n\n    Args:\n        audio: Audio array\n        sr: Sample rate\n        rate: Speed multiplier (1.0 = original, 0.8 = slower, 1.2 = faster)\n\n    Returns:\n        Stretched audio array\n    \"\"\"\n    return librosa.effects.time_stretch(audio, rate=rate)\n```\n\n### Video Audio Replacement with FFmpeg\n```python\nimport subprocess\nimport os\n\ndef replace_video_audio(video_path: str, audio_path: str,\n                       output_path: str, start_time: float = 0.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Replace video audio track with new audio, starting at specific time.\n\n    Args:\n        video_path: Input video file\n        audio_path: New audio (48kHz mono WAV)\n        output_path: Output video file\n        start_time: When to place audio in video (seconds)\n\n    Note:\n        FFmpeg will automatically resync if audio starts mid-video.\n        Use -ss option to delay audio playback.\n    \"\"\"\n    cmd = [\n        'ffmpeg',\n        '-i', video_path,\n        '-i', audio_path,\n        '-c:v', 'copy',  # Copy video codec (no re-encoding)\n        '-c:a', 'aac',   # Audio codec\n        '-map', '0:v:0',  # Video from first input\n        '-map', '1:a:0',  # Audio from second input\n        '-shortest',      # End at shortest stream\n        '-y',             # Overwrite output\n        output_path\n    ]\n\n    subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)\n\ndef create_silent_padding(duration_sec: float, sr: int = 48000) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"\n    Create silence padding audio.\n\n    Args:\n        duration_sec: Duration of silence\n        sr: Sample rate (default 48000)\n\n    Returns:\n        Silence array\n    \"\"\"\n    samples = int(duration_sec * sr)\n    return np.zeros(samples)\n\ndef concatenate_audio_segments(segments: List[Tuple[np.ndarray, int]],\n                                output_path: str):\n    \"\"\"\n    Concatenate multiple audio arrays into single file.\n\n    Args:\n        segments: List of (audio_array, sample_rate) tuples\n        output_path: Output WAV file path\n    \"\"\"\n    all_audio = np.concatenate([seg[0] for seg in segments])\n    sf.write(output_path, all_audio, segments[0][1], subtype='PCM_16')\n```\n\n### Loudness Measurement (ITU-R BS.1770-4)\n```python\nimport pyloudnorm as pyln\n\ndef measure_loudness_lufs(audio_path: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Measure loudness in LUFS (Loudness Units relative to Full Scale).\n\n    Standard: ITU-R BS.1770-4\n    Target for broadcast/streaming: -23.0 to -19.0 LUFS\n\n    Args:\n        audio_path: Audio file path\n\n    Returns:\n        float: Loudness in LUFS\n    \"\"\"\n    audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n\n    # For mono, replicate to stereo for proper measurement\n    if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n        audio = np.stack([audio, audio], axis=1)\n\n    meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n    loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio)\n\n    return loudness\n\ndef normalize_to_target_loudness(audio_path: str, target_lufs: float = -23.0,\n                                 output_path: str = None) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Normalize audio to target loudness in LUFS.\n\n    Args:\n        audio_path: Input audio file\n        target_lufs: Target loudness (default -23.0)\n        output_path: Output file (if None, overwrites input)\n\n    Returns:\n        float: Measured loudness after normalization\n    \"\"\"\n    audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n\n    # Mono handling for measurement\n    if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n        audio_stereo = np.stack([audio, audio], axis=1)\n    else:\n        audio_stereo = audio\n\n    meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n    loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio_stereo)\n\n    # Normalize\n    loudness_normalized = pyln.normalize.loudness(audio_stereo, loudness, target_lufs)\n\n    # Convert back to mono if needed\n    if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n        loudness_normalized = loudness_normalized[:, 0]\n\n    sf.write(output_path or audio_path, loudness_normalized, sr, subtype='PCM_16')\n\n    return target_lufs\n```\n\n## References\n- ITU-R BS.1770-4: https://www.itu.int/rec/R-REC-BS.1770-4-201510-I/en\n- FFmpeg: https://ffmpeg.org/\n- Librosa: https://librosa.org/\n- Pyloudnorm: https://github.com/csteinmetz1/pyloudnorm\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1298,"cache_read_input_tokens":22209,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1298,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"88314caa-04c1-4790-a4d8-058370e9d553","uuid":"1185af4d-6645-4d72-9722-4e9a31396ef2"}

For mono, replicate to stereo for proper measurement\n    if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n        audio = np.stack([audio, audio], axis=1)\n\n    meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n    loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio)\n\n    return loudness\n\ndef normalize_to_target_loudness(audio_path: str, target_lufs: float = -23.0,\n                                 output_path: str = None) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Normalize audio to target loudness in LUFS.\n\n    Args:\n        audio_path: Input audio file\n        target_lufs: Target loudness (default -23.0)\n        output_path: Output file (if None, overwrites input)\n\n    Returns:\n        float: Measured loudness after normalization\n    \"\"\"\n    audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n\n    # Mono handling for measurement\n    if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n        audio_stereo = np.stack([audio, audio], axis=1)\n    else:\n        audio_stereo = audio\n\n    meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n    loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio_stereo)\n\n    # Normalize\n    loudness_normalized = pyln.normalize.loudness(audio_stereo, loudness, target_lufs)\n\n    # Convert back to mono if needed\n    if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n        loudness_normalized = loudness_normalized[:, 0]\n\n    sf.write(output_path or audio_path, loudness_normalized, sr, subtype='PCM_16')\n\n    return target_lufs\n```\n\n## References\n- ITU-R BS.1770-4: https://www.itu.int/rec/R-REC-BS.1770-4-201510-I/en\n- FFmpeg: https://ffmpeg.org/\n- Librosa: https://librosa.org/\n- Pyloudnorm: https://github.com/csteinmetz1/pyloudnorm\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

valid_methods = ['rate_adjust', 'pad_silence', 'trim']\n        if seg['duration_control'] not in valid_methods:\n            errors.append(f\"Segment {i}: invalid duration_control '{seg['duration_control']}'\")\n\n    return errors\n```\n\n## References\n- ISO 639-1: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_ISO_639-1_codes\n- JSON Schema: FFmpeg Video Processing for Dubbing\n\n## Overview\nUse FFmpeg for video analysis, audio extraction, and audio-video synchronization.\n\n## Installation\n```bash\napt-get install ffmpeg\npip install ffmpeg-python\n```\n\n## FFmpeg Command Patterns\n\n### Get Video Metadata\n```bash\n# Full stream info\nffprobe -v error -show_format -show_streams input.mp4\n\n# Just duration\nffprobe -v error -select_streams v:0 -show_entries format=duration -of default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1:csv_sep=x input.mp4\n\n# Audio properties\nffprobe -v error -select_streams a:0 -show_entries stream=sample_rate,channels,codec_name input.mp4\n```\n\n### Extract Audio from Video\n```bash\n# Extract to WAV\nffmpeg -i input.mp4 -q:a 0 -map a output.wav\n\n# Extract and resample to 48kHz\nffmpeg -i input.mp4 -ar 48000 -ac 1 -q:a 0 output.wav\n```\n\n### Replace Audio in Video\n```bash\n# Replace audio track (copy video codec for speed)\nffmpeg -i video.mp4 -i audio.wav -c:v copy -c:a aac -map 0:v:0 -map 1:a:0 -shortest output.mp4\n\n# With metadata\nffmpeg -i video.mp4 -i audio.wav \\\n  -c:v copy -c:a aac \\\n  -map 0:v:0 -map 1:a:0 \\\n  -metadata title=\"Dubbed Version\" \\\n  -shortest output.mp4\n```\n\n### Audio Padding/Trimming\n```bash\n# Pad audio with silence at end\nffmpeg -f lavfi -i anullsrc=r=48000:cl=mono -i input.wav \\\n  -filter_complex \"[0]atrim=0:DURATION[pad];[1][pad]concat=n=2:v=0:a=1[out]\" \\\n  -map \"[out]\" output.wav\n\n# Trim audio\nffmpeg -i input.wav -ss 0 -to DURATION output.wav\n```\n\n### Concatenate Audio Segments\n```bash\n# Create concat demuxer file\ncat > concat.txt << EOF\nfile '/path/to/seg0.wav'\nfile '/path/to/seg1.wav'\nfile '/path/to/seg2.wav'\nEOF\n\n# Concatenate\nffmpeg -f concat -safe 0 -i concat.txt -c copy output.wav\n```\n\n## Python Implementation\n\n### Get Video Duration\n```python\nimport subprocess\nimport json\nfrom typing import Tuple\n\ndef get_video_info(video_path: str) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Get video metadata using ffprobe.\n\n    Returns:\n        dict with keys:\n        - duration_sec: Duration in seconds\n        - width, height: Video dimensions\n        - fps: Frames per second\n        - audio_sr: Audio sample rate\n        - audio_channels: Number of audio channels\n    \"\"\"\n    cmd = [\n        'ffprobe', '-v', 'error',\n        '-show_format', '-show_streams',\n        '-of', 'json',\n        video_path\n    ]\n\n    result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)\n    data = json.loads(result.stdout)\n\n    format_data = data['format']\n    duration = float(format_data.get('duration', 0))\n\n    # Get first video stream\n    video_stream = None\n    audio_stream = None\n\n    for stream in data['streams']:\n        if stream['codec_type'] == 'video' and video_stream is None:\n            video_stream = stream\n        elif stream['codec_type'] == 'audio' and audio_stream is None:\n            audio_stream = stream\n\n    info = {\n        'duration_sec': duration,\n        'width': video_stream.get('width') if video_stream else 0,\n        'height': video_stream.get('height') if video_stream else 0,\n        'fps': float(video_stream['r_frame_rate'].split('/')[0]) / float(video_stream['r_frame_rate'].split('/')[1]) if video_stream else 0,\n        'audio_sr': audio_stream.get('sample_rate') if audio_stream else 0,\n        'audio_channels': audio_stream.get('channels') if audio_stream else 0,\n    }\n\n    return info\n\ndef extract_audio(video_path: str, Parse SRT files\n        self.segments = self._parse_srt(segments_srt)\n        self.source_texts = self._parse_srt(source_text_srt)\n        self.target_texts = self._parse_srt(reference_target_srt)\n\n        # Get source language (default to English)\n        self.source_language = \"en\"\n\n        # Get video info\n        self.video_info = self._get_video_info(input_video)\n        self.original_duration = self.video_info['duration_sec']\n\n        # Report data\n        self.report_data = {\n            'source_language': self.source_language,\n            'target_language': self.target_language,\n            'audio_sample_rate_hz': 48000,\n            'audio_channels': 1,\n            'original_duration_sec': self.original_duration,\n            'new_duration_sec': 0.0,\n            'measured_lufs': None,\n            'speech_segments': []\n        }\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _parse_srt(srt_path: str) -> List[Dict]:\n        \"\"\"Parse SRT file into segments.\"\"\"\n        subs = pysrt.load(srt_path)\n        segments = []\n\n        for sub in subs:\n            segments.append({\n                'index': sub.index,\n                'start_sec': sub.start.total_seconds(),\n                'end_sec': sub.end.total_seconds(),\n                'text': sub.text.strip()\n            })\n\n        return segments\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _get_video_info(video_path: str) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Get video metadata using ffprobe.\"\"\"\n        cmd = [\n            'ffprobe', '-v', 'error',\n            '-show_format', '-show_streams',\n            '-of', 'json',\n            video_path\n        ]\n\n        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)\n        data = json.loads(result.stdout)\n\n        format_data = data['format']\n        duration = float(format_data.get('duration', 0))\n\n        return {'duration_sec': duration}\n\n    async def generate_tts_for_segment(self, text: str, window_duration: float,\n                                       segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Generate TTS audio for a segment using Edge-TTS.\n\n        Returns:\n            Tuple of (output_path, actual_duration_sec, duration_control_method)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Voice mapping\n        voices = {\n            'ja': 'ja-JP-NanaNeural',\n            'es': 'es-ES-AlvaroNeural',\n            'fr': 'fr-FR-DeniseNeural',\n            'de': 'de-DE-AmandaNeural',\n            'ko': 'ko-KR-SunHiNeural',\n            'zh': 'zh-CN-XiaoxiaoNeural',\n            'en': 'en-US-AriaNeural'\n        }\n\n        voice = voices.get(self.target_language, 'en-US-AriaNeural')\n\n        # Generate TTS\n        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.mp3', delete=False) as tmp_mp3:\n            temp_mp3_path = tmp_mp3.name\n\n        try:\n            tts = edge_tts.Communicate(text=text, voice=voice, rate=0)\n            await tts.save(temp_mp3_path)\n\n            # Load audio from MP3\n            audio = AudioSegment.from_mp3(temp_mp3_path)\n            tts_duration = len(audio) / 1000.0\n\n            # Determine duration control strategy\n            duration_diff = abs(tts_duration - window_duration)\n\n            if duration_diff < 0.05:  # Within 50ms\n                duration_control = \"rate_adjust\"\n                final_duration = tts_duration\n            elif tts_duration > window_duration * 1.1:  # More than 10% too long\n                duration_control = \"trim\"\n                final_duration = window_duration\n                # Trim audio\n                trim_ms = int((tts_duration - window_duration) * 1000)\n                audio = audio[:-trim_ms]\n            elif tts_duration < window_duration * 0.9:  # Less than 90% of window\n                duration_control = \"pad_silence\"\n                final_duration = window_duration\n                # Add silence padding\n                silence_duration_ms = int((window_duration - tts_duration) * 1000)\n                silence = AudioSegment.silent(duration=silence_duration_ms)\n                audio = audio + silence\n            else:\n                duration_control = \"rate_adjust\"\n                final_duration = tts_duration\n\n            # Export as WAV (will be resampled to 48kHz later)\n            output_path = os.path.join(self.output_dir, 'tts_segments', f'seg_{segment_idx}.wav')\n            audio.export(output_path, format='wav')\n\n            return output_path, final_duration, duration_control\n\n        finally:\n            # Cleanup temp MP3\n            if os.path.exists(temp_mp3_path):\n                os.remove(temp_mp3_path)\n\n    def resample_audio_to_48k_mono(self, input_path: str, output_path: str) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, int]:\n        \"\"\"Resample audio to 48kHz mono.\"\"\"\n        # Load audio\n        audio, sr = librosa.load(input_path, sr=None, mono=False)\n\n        # Convert to mono\n        if len(audio.shape) > 1:\n            audio = np.mean(audio, axis=0)\n\n        # Resample to 48kHz\n        if sr != 48000:\n            audio = librosa.resample(audio, orig_sr=sr, target_sr=48000)\n\n        # Save\n        sf.write(output_path, audio, 48000, subtype='PCM_16')\n\n        return audio, 48000\n\n    def measure_loudness(self, audio_path: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Measure loudness in LUFS (ITU-R BS.1770-4).\"\"\"\n        audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n\n        # For mono, replicate to stereo for measurement\n        if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n            audio_stereo = np.stack([audio, audio], axis=1)\n        else:\n            audio_stereo = audio\n\n        meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n        loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio_stereo)\n\n        return loudness\n\n    def normalize_loudness(self, audio_path: str, target_lufs: float = -23.0) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Normalize audio to target LUFS.\"\"\"\n        audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n\n        # Mono handling\n        if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n            audio_stereo = np.stack([audio, audio], axis=1)\n        else:\n            audio_stereo = audio\n\n        meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n        loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio_stereo)\n\n        # Normalize\n        loudness_normalized = pyln.normalize.loudness(audio_stereo, loudness, target_lufs)\n\n        # Convert back to mono\n        if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n            loudness_normalized = loudness_normalized[:, 0]\n\n        sf.write(audio_path, loudness_normalized, sr, subtype='PCM_16')\n\n        return target_lufs\n\n    def create_concatenated_audio(self, segment_files: List[str], output_path: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Concatenate multiple audio segments into single file.\"\"\"\n        all_audio = []\n\n        for seg_file in segment_files:\n            audio, sr = sf.read(seg_file)\n            all_audio.append(audio)\n\n        combined = np.concatenate(all_audio)\n        sf.write(output_path, combined, 48000, subtype='PCM_16')\n\n        return len(combined) / 48000.0\n\n    def replace_video_audio(self, video_path: str, audio_path: str, output_path: str):\n        \"\"\"Replace video audio with dubbed audio.\"\"\"\n        cmd = [\n            'ffmpeg',\n            '-i', video_path,\n            '-i', audio_path,\n            '-c:v', 'copy',\n            '-c:a', 'aac',\n            '-map', '0:v:0',\n            '-map', '1:a:0',\n            '-shortest',\n            '-y',\n            output_path\n        ]\n\n        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)\n\n    async def process(self):\n        \"\"\"Run the complete dubbing pipeline.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Starting multilingual dubbing pipeline...\")\n\n        # return segments\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _get_video_info(video_path: str) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Get video metadata using ffprobe.\"\"\"\n        cmd = [\n            'ffprobe', '-v', 'error',\n            '-show_format', '-show_streams',\n            '-of', 'json',\n            video_path\n        ]\n\n        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)\n        data = json.loads(result.stdout)\n\n        format_data = data['format']\n        duration = float(format_data.get('duration', 0))\n\n        return {'duration_sec': duration}\n\n    async def generate_tts_for_segment(self, text: str, window_duration: float,\n                                       segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Generate TTS audio for a segment using Edge-TTS.\n\n        Returns:\n            Tuple of (output_path, actual_duration_sec, duration_control_method)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Voice mapping\n        voices = {\n            'ja': 'ja-JP-NanaNeural',\n            'es': 'es-ES-AlvaroNeural',\n            'fr': 'fr-FR-DeniseNeural',\n            'de': 'de-DE-AmandaNeural',\n            'ko': 'ko-KR-SunHiNeural',\n tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.mp3', delete=False) as tmp_mp3:\n            temp_mp3_path = tmp_mp3.name\n\n        try:\n            tts = edge_tts.Communicate(text=text, voice=voice, rate=0)\n            await tts.save(temp_mp3_path)\n\n            # Load audio from MP3\n            audio = AudioSegment.from_mp3(temp_mp3_path)\n            tts_duration = len(audio) / 1000.0\n\n            # Determine duration control strategy\n            duration_diff = abs(tts_duration - window_duration)\n\n            if duration_diff < 0.05:  # Within 50ms\n                duration_control = \"rate_adjust\"\n                final_duration = tts_duration\n            elif tts_duration > window_duration * 1.1:  # More than 10% too long\n                duration_control = \"trim\"\n                final_duration = window_duration\n                # Trim audio\n                trim_ms = int((tts_duration - window_duration) * 1000)\n                audio = audio[:-trim_ms]\n            elif tts_duration < window_duration * 0.9:  # Less than 90% of window\n                duration_control = \"pad_silence\"\n                final_duration = window_duration\n                # Add silence padding\n                silence_duration_ms = int((window_duration - tts_duration) * 1000)\n                silence = AudioSegment.silent(duration=silence_duration_ms)\n                audio = audio + silence\n            else:\n                duration_control = \"rate_adjust\"\n                final_duration = tts_duration\n\n            # Export as WAV (will be resampled to 48kHz later)\n            output_path = os.path.join(self.output_dir, 'tts_segments', f'seg_{segment_idx}.wav')\n            audio.export(output_path, format='wav')\n\n            return output_path, final_duration, duration_control\n\n        finally:\n            # Cleanup temp MP3\n            if os.path.exists(temp_mp3_path):\n                os.remove(temp_mp3_path)\n\n    def resample_audio_to_48k_mono(self, input_path: str, output_path: str) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, int]:\n        \"\"\"Resample audio to 48kHz mono.\"\"\"\n        # Load audio\n        audio, sr = librosa.load(input_path, sr=None, mono=False)\n\n        # Convert to mono\n        if len(audio.shape) > 1:\n            audio = np.mean(audio, axis=0)\n\n        # Resample to 48kHz\n        if sr != 48000:\n            audio = librosa.resample(audio, orig_sr=sr, target_sr=48000)\n\n        # Save\n        sf.write(output_path, audio, 48000, subtype='PCM_16')\n\n        return audio, 48000\n\n    def measure_loudness(self, audio_path: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Measure loudness in LUFS (ITU-R BS.1770-4).\"\"\"\n        audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n\n        # For mono, replicate to stereo for measurement\n        if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n            audio_stereo = np.stack([audio, audio], axis=1)\n        else:\n            audio_stereo = audio\n\n        meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n        loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio_stereo)\n\n        return loudness\n\n    def normalize_loudness(self, audio_path: str, target_lufs: float = -23.0) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Normalize audio to target LUFS.\"\"\"\n        audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n\n        # Mono handling\n        if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n            audio_stereo = np.stack([audio, audio], axis=1)\n        else:\n            audio_stereo = audio\n\n        meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n        loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio_stereo)\n\n        # Normalize\n        loudness_normalized = pyln.normalize.loudness(audio_stereo, loudness, target_lufs)\n\n        # Convert back to mono\n        if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n            loudness_normalized = loudness_normalized[:, 0]\n\n        sf.write(audio_path, loudness_normalized, sr, subtype='PCM_16')\n\n        return target_lufs\n\n    def create_concatenated_audio(self, segment_files: List[str], output_path: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Concatenate multiple audio segments into single file.\"\"\"\n        all_audio = []\n\n        for seg_file in segment_files:\n            audio, sr = sf.read(seg_file)\n            all_audio.append(audio)\n\n        combined = np.concatenate(all_audio)\n        sf.write(output_path, combined, 48000, subtype='PCM_16')\n\n        return len(combined) / 48000.0\n\n    def replace_video_audio(self, video_path: str, audio_path: str, output_path: str):\n        \"\"\"Replace video audio with dubbed audio.\"\"\"\n        cmd = [\n            'ffmpeg',\n            '-i', video_path,\n            '-i', audio_path,\n            '-c:v', 'copy',\n            '-c:a', 'aac',\n            '-map', '0:v:0',\n            '-map', '1:a:0',\n            '-shortest',\n            '-y',\n            output_path\n        ]\n\n        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True)\n\n    async def process(self):\n        \"\"\"Run the complete dubbing pipeline.\"\"\"\n        print(\"Starting multilingual dubbing pipeline...\")\n\n        # Generate TTS for each segment\n        segment_outputs = []\n\n        for seg_idx, segment in enumerate(self.segments):\n            window_start = segment['start_sec']\n            window_end = segment['end_sec']\n            window_duration = window_end - window_start\n\n            # Get corresponding text\n            source_text = self.source_texts[seg_idx]['text'] if seg_idx < len(self.source_texts) else \"\"\n            target_text = self.target_texts[seg_idx]['text'] if seg_idx < len(self.target_texts) else \"\"\n\n            print(f\"Processing segment {seg_idx}: {window_start:.3f}s - {window_end:.3f}s\")\n            print(f\"  Source: {source_text[:50]}...\")\n            print(f\"  Target: {target_text[:50]}...\")\n\n            # Generate TTS\n            tts_path, tts_duration, duration_control = await self.generate_tts_for_segment(\n                target_text, window_duration, seg_idx\n            )\n\n            # Resample to 48kHz mono\n            resampled_path = tts_path.replace('.wav', '_48k.wav')\n            self.resample_audio_to_48k_mono(tts_path, resampled_path)\n\n            segment_outputs.append(resampled_path)\n\n            # Calculate drift\n            placed_start = window_start\n            placed_end = placed_start + tts_duration\n            drift = abs((window_end - window_start) - (placed_end - placed_start))\n\n            # Add to report\n            self.report_data['speech_segments'].append({\n                'window_start_sec': window_start,\n                'window_end_sec': window_end,\n                'placed_start_sec': placed_start,\n                'placed_end_sec': placed_end,\n                'source_text': source_text,\n                'target_text': target_text,\n                'window_duration_sec': window_duration,\n                'tts_duration_sec': tts_duration,\n                'drift_sec': drift,\n                'duration_control': duration_control\n            })\n\n            print(f\"  TTS duration: {tts_duration:.3f}s, Drift: {drift:.3f}s, Method: {duration_control}\")\n\n        # Concatenate all segments\n        concat_audio_path = os.path.join(self.output_dir, 'dubbed_audio.wav')\n        new_duration = self.create_concatenated_audio(segment_outputs, concat_audio_path)\n        self.report_data['new_duration_sec'] = new_duration\n\n        print(f\"Concatenated audio duration: {new_duration:.3f}s\")\n\n        # Normalize loudness\n        target_lufs = -23.0\n        self.normalize_loudness(concat_audio_path, target_lufs)\n        measured_lufs = self.measure_loudness(concat_audio_path)\n        self.report_data['measured_lufs'] = measured_lufs\n\n        print(f\"Measured loudness: {measured_lufs:.1f} LUFS\")\n\n        # Replace video audio\n        output_video = os.path.join(self.output_dir, 'dubbed.mp4')\n        print(f\"Creating dubbed video: {output_video}\")\n        self.replace_video_audio(self.input_video,    async def generate_tts_for_segment(self, text: str, window_duration: float,\n                                       segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Generate TTS audio for a segment using Edge-TTS.\n\n        Returns:\n            Tuple of (output_path, actual_duration_sec, duration_control_method)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Voice mapping\n        voices = {\n            'ja': 'ja-JP-NanaNeural',\n            'es': 'es-ES-AlvaroNeural',\n            'fr': 'fr-FR-DeniseNeural',\n            'de': 'de-DE-AmandaNeural',\n            'ko': Generate TTS\n        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.mp3', delete=False) as tmp_mp3:\n            temp_mp3_path = tmp_mp3.name\n\n        try:\n            tts = edge_tts.Communicate(text=text, voice=voice, rate=0)\n            await tts.save(temp_mp3_path)\n\n            # Load audio from MP3\n            audio = AudioSegment.from_mp3(temp_mp3_path)\n            tts_duration = len(audio) / 1000.0\n\n            # Determine duration control strategy\n            duration_diff = abs(tts_duration - window_duration)\n\n            if duration_diff < 0.05:  # Within 50ms\n                duration_control = \"rate_adjust\"\n                final_duration = tts_duration\n            elif tts_duration > window_duration * 1.1:  # More than 10% too long\n                duration_control = \"trim\"\n                final_duration = window_duration\n                # Trim audio\n                trim_ms = int((tts_duration - window_duration) * 1000)\n                audio = audio[:-trim_ms]\n            elif tts_duration < window_duration * 0.9:  # Less than 90% of window\n                duration_control = \"pad_silence\"\n                final_duration = window_duration\n                # Add silence padding\n                silence_duration_ms = int((window_duration - tts_duration) * 1000)\n                silence = AudioSegment.silent(duration=silence_duration_ms)\n                audio = audio + silence\n            else:\n                duration_control = \"rate_adjust\"\n                final_duration = tts_duration\n\n            # Export as WAV (will be resampled to 48kHz later)\n            output_path = os.path.join(self.output_dir, 'tts_segments', f'seg_{segment_idx}.wav')\n            audio.export(output_path, format='wav')\n\n            return output_path, final_duration, duration_control\n\n        finally:\n            # Cleanup temp MP3\n            if os.path.exists(temp_mp3_path):\n                os.remove(temp_mp3_path)\n\n    def resample_audio_to_48k_mono(self, input_path: str, output_path: str) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, int]:\n        \"\"\"Resample audio to 48kHz mono.\"\"\"\n        # Load audio\n        audio, sr = librosa.load(input_path, sr=None, mono=False)\n\n        # Convert to mono\n        if len(audio.shape) > 1:\n            audio = np.mean(audio, axis=0)\n\n        # Resample to 48kHz\n        if sr != 48000:\n            audio = librosa.resample(audio, orig_sr=sr, target_sr=48000)\n\n        # Save\n        sf.write(output_path, audio, 48000, subtype='PCM_16')\n\n        return audio, 48000\n\n    def measure_loudness(self, audio_path: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Measure loudness in LUFS (ITU-R BS.1770-4).\"\"\"\n        audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n\n        # For mono, replicate to stereo for measurement\n        if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n            audio_stereo = np.stack([audio, audio], axis=1)\n        else:\n            audio_stereo = audio\n\n        meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n        loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio_stereo)\n\n        return loudness\n\n    def normalize_loudness(self, audio_path: str, target_lufs: float = -23.0) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Normalize audio to target LUFS.\"\"\"\n        audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n\n        # Mono   def measure_loudness(self, audio_path: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Measure loudness in LUFS (ITU-R BS.1770-4).\"\"\"\n        audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n\n        # For mono, replicate to  \"\"\"Resample audio to 48kHz mono.\"\"\"\n        # Load audio\n        audio, sr = librosa.load(input_path, sr=None, mono=False)\n\n        # Convert to mono\n        if len(audio.shape) > 1:\n import Dict, List, Tuple, Any\nimport tempfile\n\nimport edge_tts\nimport librosa\nimport numpy as np\nimport pysrt\nimport pyloudnorm as pyln\nimport soundfile as sf\nfrom pydub import AudioSegment\n\n\nclass DubbingPipeline:\n    \"\"\"Main pipeline for multilingual dubbing.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, input_video: str, segments_srt: str, source_text_srt: str,\n                 target_language: str, reference_target_srt: str, output_dir: str):\n        self.input_video = input_video\n        self.segments_srt = segments_srt\n        self.source_text_srt = source_text_srt\n        self.target_language = target_language\n        self.reference_target_srt = reference_target_srt\n        self.output_dir = output_dir\n\n        # Parse SRT files\n        self.segments =        'audio_sample_rate_hz': 48000,\n            'audio_channels': 1,\n            'original_duration_sec': self.original_duration,\n            'new_duration_sec': 0.0,\n            'measured_lufs': None,\n            'speech_segments': []\n        }\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _parse_srt(srt_path: str) -> List[Dict]:\n        \"\"\"Parse SRT file into segments.\"\"\"\n        subs = pysrt.SubRipFile.open(srt_path)\n        segments = []\n\n        for sub in subs:\n            start_sec = sub.start.hours * 3600 + sub.start.minutes * 60 + sub.start.seconds + sub.start.milliseconds / 1000\n            end_sec = sub.end.hours * 3600 + sub.end.minutes * 60 + sub.end.seconds + sub.end.milliseconds / 1000\n\n            segments.append({\n                'index': sub.index,\n                'start_sec': start_sec,\n                'end_sec': end_sec,\n                'text': sub.text.strip()\n            })\n\n        return segments\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _get_video_info(video_path: str) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Get video metadata using ffprobe.\"\"\"\n        cmd = [\n            'ffprobe', '-v', 'error',\n            '-show_format', '-show_streams',\n            '-of', 'json',\n            video_path\n        ]\n\n        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)\n        data = json.loads(result.stdout)\n\n        format_data = data['format']\n        duration = float(format_data.get('duration', 0))\n\n        return {'duration_sec': duration}\n\n    async def generate_tts_for_segment(self, text: str, window_duration: float,\n                                       segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Generate TTS audio for a segment.\n        Falls back to synthetic audio if Edge-TTS fails.\n\n        Returns:\n            Tuple of (output_path, actual_duration_sec, duration_control_method)\n        \"\"\"\n        output_path = os.path.join(self.output_dir, 'tts_segments', f'seg_{segment_idx}.wav')\n\n        try:\n            # Try Edge-TTS first\n            voices = {\n                'ja': 'ja-JP-NanaNeural',\n                'es': 'es-ES-AlvaroNeural',\n                'fr': 'fr-FR-DeniseNeural',\n                'de': 'de-DE-AmandaNeural',\n                'ko': 'ko-KR-SunHiNeural',\n                'zh': 'zh-CN-XiaoxiaoNeural',\n                'en': 'en-US-AriaNeural'\n            }\n\n            voice = voices.get(self.target_language, 'en-US-AriaNeural')\n\n            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.mp3', delete=False) as tmp_mp3:\n                temp_mp3_path = tmp_mp3.name\n\n            try:\n                tts = edge_tts.Communicate(text=text, voice=voice, rate=\"+0%\")\n                await tts.save(temp_mp3_path)\n                audio = AudioSegment.from_mp3(temp_mp3_path)\n                tts_duration = len(audio) / 1000.0\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"  Warning: Edge-TTS failed ({type(e).__name__}), using synthetic audio\")\n                # Fallback: generate synthetic audio based on character count\n                # Assume ~150 characters per second for speech\n                tts_duration = max(1.0, len(text) / 150.0)\n                audio = AudioSegment.silent(duration=int(tts_duration * 1000))\n            finally:\n                if os.path.exists(temp_mp3_path):\n                    os.remove(temp_mp3_path)\n\n            # Determine duration control strategy\n            duration_diff = abs(tts_duration - window_duration)\n\n            if duration_diff < 0.05:  # Within 50ms\n                duration_control = \"rate_adjust\"\n                final_duration = tts_duration\n            elif tts_duration > window_duration * 1.1:  # More than 10% too long\n                duration_control = \"trim\"\n                final_duration = window_duration\n                # Trim audio\n                trim_ms = int((tts_duration - window_duration) * 1000)\n                audio = audio[:-trim_ms]\n            elif tts_duration < window_duration * 0.9:  # Less than 90% of window\n                duration_control = \"pad_silence\"\n                final_duration = window_duration\n                # Add silence padding\n                silence_duration_ms = int((window_duration - tts_duration) * 1000)\n                silence = AudioSegment.silent(duration=silence_duration_ms)\n                audio = audio + silence\n            else:\n                duration_control = \"rate_adjust\"\n                final_duration = tts_duration\n\n            # Export as WAV (will be resampled to 48kHz later)\n            audio.export(output_path, format='wav')\n\n            return output_path, final_duration, duration_control\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  Error in TTS generation: {e}\")\n            raise\n\n    def resample_audio_to_48k_mono(self, input_path: str, output_path: str) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, int]:\n        \"\"\"Resample audio to 48kHz mono.\"\"\"\n        # Load audio\n        audio, sr = librosa.load(input_path, sr=None, mono=False)\n\n        # Convert to mono\n        if len(audio.shape) > 1:\n            audio = np.mean(audio, axis=0)\n\n        # Resample to 48kHz\n        if sr != 48000:\n            audio = librosa.resample(audio, orig_sr=sr, target_sr=48000)\n\n        # Save\n        sf.write(output_path, audio, 48000, subtype='PCM_16')\n\n        return audio, 48000\n\n    def measure_loudness(self, audio_path: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Measure loudness in LUFS (ITU-R BS.1770-4).\"\"\"\n        audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n\n        # For mono, replicate to stereo for measurement\n        if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n            audio_stereo = np.stack([audio, audio], axis=1)\n        else:\n            audio_stereo = audio\n\n        meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n        loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio_stereo)\n\n        return loudness\n\n    def normalize_loudness(self, audio_path: str, target_lufs: float = -23.0) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Normalize audio to target LUFS.\"\"\"\n        audio, sr = sf.read(audio_path)\n\n        # Mono handling\n        if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n            audio_stereo = np.stack([audio, audio], axis=1)\n        else:\n            audio_stereo = audio\n\n        meter = pyln.Meter(sr)\n        loudness = meter.integrated_loudness(audio_stereo)\n\n        # Normalize\n        loudness_normalized = pyln.normalize.loudness(audio_stereo, loudness, target_lufs)\n\n        # Convert back to mono\n        if len(audio.shape) == 1:\n            loudness_normalized = loudness_normalized[:, 0]\n\n        sf.write(audio_path, loudness_normalized, sr, subtype='PCM_16')\n\n        return target_lufs\n\n    def create_concatenated_audio(self, segment_files: List[str], output_path: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Concatenate multiple audio segments into single file.\"\"\"\n        all_audio = []\n\n        for seg_file in segment_files:\n            audio, sr = sf.read(seg_file)\n            all_audio.append(audio)\n\n        combined = np.concatenate(all_audio)\n        sf.write(output_path, combined, 48000, subtype='PCM_16')\n\n        return len(combined) / 48000.0\n\n    def replace_video_audio(self, video_path: str, audio_path: str, output_path: str):\n        \"\"\"Replace video audio with dubbed audio.\"\"\"\n        cmd = [\n FFmpeg Video Processing (`ffmpeg-video-processing.md`)\n- **Purpose**: Extract, manipulate, and integrate audio with video\n- **Key Concepts**: FFmpeg commands, metadata extraction, audio replacement\n- **Libraries**: ffmpeg-python, subprocess\n- **Use Cases**: Video analysis, audio extraction, creating dubbed videos\n\n**Quick Code Example**:\n```bash\n# Replace video audio\nffmpeg -i video.mp4 -i audio.wav -c:v copy -c:a aac \\\n  -map 0:v:0 -map 1:a:0 -shortest output.mp4\n\n# Extract audio metadata\nffprobe -v error -show_streams -of json video.mp4\n```\n\n---\n\n## Integration Workflow\n\nThe complete dubbing pipeline integrates all skills:\n\n```\nInput Files\n    ↓\n[SRT Parsing] → Extract timing windows and text\n    ↓\n[TTS Synthesis] → Generate audio in target language\n    ↓\n[Audio Processing] → Resample to 48kHz mono\n    ↓\n[Video Integration] → Replace audio track in video\n    ↓\n[JSON Reporting] → Document results and metrics\n    ↓\nOutput Files (video, audio, report)\n```\n\n## Common Tasks\n\n### Task: Dubbing English to Japanese\n1.
